#### Rule: [heading-order](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.12/heading-order?application=playwright)
**Impact**: moderate

- First identified: 2026-09-14
- Message: Heading levels should only increase by one
- HTML: `<h4 class="styles_title__UllnM">Verspilling is killing, dus iedereen past op de buurtkoelkast</h4>`
- XPath: `.styles_wrapTeaserCard__zxdB6:nth-child(1) > .styles_cardWrap__jCCQd.styles_contentGrid__B7Vnk[rel="noopener"] > .styles_textWrapper__qrNXi > h4`

**Impact**: moderate
